row"><div class="jsx-a9265aa7308d2caa col-md-12 ipo-summary"><div class="jsx-a9265aa7308d2caa d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center flex-wrap"><h2 class="jsx-a9265aa7308d2caa section-title mb-0">About Paras Healthcare Ltd.</h2><span class="jsx-a9265aa7308d2caa text-muted small fw-normal ms-auto">Updated on <!-- -->Oct 29, 2026</span></div><div id="ipoSummary" class="jsx-a9265aa7308d2caa collapse "><p>Incorporated in1987, Paras Healthcare Limited is a leading clinical specialty-led hospital platform providing tertiary and quaternary healthcare services across North India, Bihar, and Jharkhand under the "Paras Health" brand. The company operates eight hospitals with an aggregate bed capacity of 2,211 beds across Gurugram, Panchkula, Patna, Darbhanga, Kanpur, Udaipur, Ranchi, and Srinagar as of June 30, 2026. </p>
<p>It specializes in cardiac sciences, oncology, neurosciences, gastro sciences, orthopedics &amp; sports injury, and renal sciences (CONGOR specialties), which contributed 75.30% of revenue in Fiscal 2026.</p>
<p>Paras Healthcare has over 20 years of operating history and follows a balanced growth strategy through mature, emerging, and new hospitals. The company plans to expand its network with a 300-bed hospital in Gurugram and a 500-bed hospital in Ludhiana, increasing total capacity to 3,011 beds by March 2028.</p>
<p>As of March 31, 2026, it had 4,670 employees, including 1,084 doctors and 1,937 nurses.</p>
<p><strong>Strengths</strong></p>
<ul>
<li>Leading healthcare provider in North India, Bihar, and Jharkhand.</li>
<li>Network of 8 hospitals with 2,211-bed capacity.</li>
<li>Strong Paras Health brand with over 20 years of experience.</li>
<li>Focus on high-acuity CONGOR specialties.</li>
<li>Balanced portfolio of mature, emerging, and new hospitals.</li>
<li>Strong occupancy growth and operational efficiency.</li>
<li>Experienced workforce of 1,011 doctors and 1,665 nurses.</li>
<li>Expansion pipeline to reach 3,011 beds by FY28.</li>
<li>Advanced clinical infrastructure and standardized care protocols.</li>
</ul></div><div class="jsx-a9265aa7308d2caa float-end"><button id="anchorReadText" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="ipoSummary" aria-label="Expand details" type="button" class="jsx-a9265aa7308d2caa collapsed  btn btn-link mb-0">+ Read More</button></div></div></div></div><div class="col-12 mb-3"><div class="card p-3 border rounded-4 shadow-sm equal-height"><div
